- **phonics-decoding**: Letter-sound relationships, decoding strategies, and word-attack skills for reading unfamiliar text. Covers alphabetic principle, phoneme-grapheme correspondences (single letters, digraphs, diphthongs, r-controlled vowels), syllable types (closed, open, VCe, vowel team, r-controlled, consonant-le), morphemic analysis (prefixes, suffixes, roots), multisyllabic word strategies, and fluency development through automaticity. Use when teaching decoding, diagnosing reading errors, analyzing miscue patterns, or building word-attack routines. - **phonon**: - Computing phonon band structures and phonon density of states - Checking dynamical stability of a crystal structure (imaginary frequencies = unstable) - Obtaining thermodynamic properties: heat capacity (Cv), entropy (S), Helmholtz free energy (F) vs temperature - Validating a relaxed structure before further calculations - Comparing lattice dynamics from MLIP vs DFT ([bjzgcai/MatClaw](https://github.com/bjzgcai/MatClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/thermal-properties/phonon) · ★5)
- **phonon-from-outcar**: - Extracting phonon frequencies and eigenvectors from a VASP OUTCAR produced by IBRION=5,6,7,8 (finite differences or DFPT) - Post-processing QE dynmat.x or matdyn.x output files for phonon band structures and DOS - Computing element-projected (atom-projected) phonon band structure and DOS - Sorting phonon bands by eigenvector continuity to avoid band-crossing artifacts in plots - Parsing Born eff ([bjzgcai/MatClaw](https://github.com/bjzgcai/MatClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/thermal-properties/phonon-from-outcar) · ★5)
- **phononic-crystal-design**: Research and design acoustic, elastic and phononic crystals: 声子晶体设计、腔管第一原理建模、带隙与逆向设计、制造鲁棒性、平带/BCP、Dirac/Weyl/nodal rings、HOTI、非厄米与合成维。Use for mechanism selection, Hamiltonian-to-geometry mapping, generalized eigenproblems, signed/long-range coupling, topological invariants, 3D COMSOL specifications, experimental diagnosis and falsifiable research hypotheses. Draws on 37 distinct papers with version/page provenance; load only relevant methods. ([tsukimori-dev/phononic-crystal-design](https://github.com/tsukimori-dev/phononic-crystal-design/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **phonopy**: General phonon-workflow skill built around phonopy, independent of force backend. USE WHEN you need to prepare finite-displacement phonon calculations, build force constants, and analyze phonon properties (band structure, DOS, thermal quantities) while obtaining forces from different engines such as VASP, Quantum ESPRESSO, or ML force fields. ([jinzhezenggroup/computational-chemistry-agent-skills](https://github.com/jinzhezenggroup/computational-chemistry-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/analysis/phonopy) · ★161)
- **phonopy-interface**: - You want to use phonopy as the phonon post-processor with QE or VASP as the force engine. - You need displaced supercell structures for QE `pw.x` or VASP force calculations. - You have completed force calculations and need to import results back into phonopy. - You want phonon band structures, DOS, thermal properties, or interatomic force constant (IFC) analysis. - You need the normalized trace ([bjzgcai/MatClaw](https://github.com/bjzgcai/MatClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/code-interfaces/phonopy-interface) · ★5)
- **phonopy-kpath**: - You are running a phonopy phonon band structure calculation and need the k-path settings. - You want to generate `band.conf` or `KPATH.in` for phonopy. - You need seekpath-consistent k-paths for phonon dispersion plots. - You want to compare phonon band structures computed with different codes (QE matdyn.x, phonopy, VASP DFPT) using the same k-path. ([bjzgcai/MatClaw](https://github.com/bjzgcai/MatClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/kpath-utilities/phonopy-kpath) · ★5)

- **phopile-eval**: This benchmark evaluates foundation models' ability to solve Olympiad-level physics problems using retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). It probes step-wise logical reasoning, correct application of physical laws, and robustness to noisy retrieved context. Performance is measured via an LLM-as-judge scoring framework that rewards both intermediate reasoning quality and final answer correctness. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PhoPile, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Average Score (AS). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/phopile-eval) · ★3)
